
## Asset Maps (4 endpoints)

### GET /v3/assetmaps/surveys
Summary: Get multiple domain surveys
Parameters:
  query user_id: string — specify a user_id (if permitted)
  query tenant_view: boolean — combine with user_id for tenant scope (if permitted))
  query survey_id: string — Survey ID
  query domain: string — Domain
  query page: integer — Page number (1-based)
  query limit: integer default=20 max=1000 — Number of surveys to retrieve
Responses:
  200: Domain Surveys Found → {data: array<{domain: string, survey_id: string, schedule_id: string, user_id: string, tracked_subdomains: array<string>, subdomains_checked: integer, churn_count: integer, dns_ips: array<string>, created_at: string, completed_at: string, status: string, callback: {start: {url: string, method: string, attempts: array<{time: string, code: integer, raw: string, error: string}>, complete: boolean}, success: {url: string, method: string, attempts: array<{time: string, code: integer, raw: string, error: string}>, complete: boolean}, fail: {url: string, method: string, attempts: array<{time: string, code: integer, raw: string, error: string}>, complete: boolean}}}>, pagination: {limit: integer, page: integer, total: integer, total_pages: integer, has_next: boolean, has_prev: boolean, next_cursor: string, prev_cursor: string}}
  400: Invalid Limit → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  500: Failed to Get Domain Surveys → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

## Captures (11 endpoints)

### GET /v3/captures
Summary: Get all capture requests for a user or tenant
Parameters:
  query user_id: string — specify a user_id (if permitted)
  query tenant_view: boolean — combine with user_id for tenant scope (if permitted))
  query capture_id: string — get capture requests for a specific capture_id
  query page: integer — Page number (1-based)
  query limit: integer default=100 max=1000 — Number of Capture Requests to Retrieve
Responses:
  200: Capture Requests → {data: array<{capture_id: string, schedule_id: string, user_id: string, url: string, status: string, created_at: string, attempted_at: string, completed_at: string, callback: {start: {url: string, method: string, attempts: array<{time: string, code: integer, raw: string, error: string}>, complete: boolean}, success: {url: string, method: string, attempts: array<{time: string, code: integer, raw: string, error: string}>, complete: boolean}, fail: {url: string, method: string, attempts: array<{time: string, code: integer, raw: string, error: string}>, complete: boolean}}, cookie_count: integer, network_log_count: integer, header_count: integer, screenshot_size: integer, deleted: boolean, region: string, country: string, territory: string}>, pagination: {limit: integer, page: integer, total: integer, total_pages: integer, has_next: boolean, has_prev: boolean, next_cursor: string, prev_cursor: string}}
  400: Invalid Request ID → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

### GET /v3/permutations
Summary: Get a list of permutation checks
Parameters:
  query user_id: string — specify a user_id (if permitted)
  query tenant_view: boolean — combine with user_id for tenant scope (if permitted))
  query check_id: string — Check ID
  query domain: string — Domain
  query status: string — Status
  query page: integer — Page number (1-based)
  query limit: integer — Limit (max 1000, default 100)

### GET /v3/rules/{rule_id}/matches
Summary: Preview rule matches
Description: Loads a caller-owned user rule, queries the global results index for candidates, then explains each candidate in-memory. Mongo-only false positives are dropped. Always returns at most 20 explained matches.

  path rule_id: string [required] — Rule ID
  query type: string default=scan enum=['scan', 'url', 'dns'] — Result type: scan, url, or dns
  query user_id: string — specify a user_id (if permitted)
  query tenant_view: boolean — combine with user_id for tenant scope (if permitted)
Responses:
  200: Matching results with match_report (max 20) → {data: {type: string, rule_id: string, count: integer, results: array<{document: object, match_report: {hits: array<{end_index: integer, field: string, header_name: string, matched_text: string, operator: string, path: string, query_index: integer, raw_line_index: integer, rule_text: string, start_index: integer, value_index: integer}>, matched: boolean, reason: string, tags: array<string>}}>}, links: {}}
  400: Invalid type or rule cannot be evaluated for that type → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

## Search (7 endpoints)

### GET /v3/search/advanced/dns
Summary: Full-text search DNS results
Description: Performs linguistic full-text search across DNS result fields with BM25 relevance scoring.  **Search Features:** - Full-text search with stemming and linguistic analysis - BM25 relevance scoring (results ordered by relevance) - Cursor-based pagination with O(1) performance  **Query Examples:** - `?q=cloudflare google` - Find results containing cloudflare OR google - `?q=\"exact phrase\"` - Find exact phrase matches - `?q=nameserver&fields=ns,txt` - Search specific fields only  **Pagination:** - ...
Parameters:
  query q: string [required] — Search query - Full-text search across fields
  query fields: string — Comma-separated fields to search (default: all fields) - Example: a,ns,txt,mx
  query limit: integer — Results per page (default: 20, max: 100)
  query search_after: string — Pagination token from previous response (base64-encoded string)
  query domain: string — Filter by exact domain - Example: example.com
  query a: string — Filter by A record - Example: 192.168.1.1
  query aaaa: string — Filter by AAAA record - Example: 2001:0db8:85a3:0000:0000:8a2e:0370:7334
  query caa: string — Filter by CAA record - Example: letsencrypt.org
  query cname: string — Filter by CNAME record - Example: www.example.com
  query dmarc: string — Filter by DMARC record - Example: v=DMARC1
  query mx: string — Filter by MX record - Example: mail.example.com
  query ns: string — Filter by NS record - Example: ns1.example.com
  query spf: string — Filter by SPF record - Example: v=spf1
  query srv: string — Filter by SRV record - Example: _service._proto.name
  query txt: string — Filter by TXT record - Example: verification-token
  query min_date: string — Filter by minimum checked_at date - ISO 8601 format: 2024-01-01T00:00:00Z
Responses:
  200: Search results with pagination metadata → {data: array<{domain: string, checked_at: string, tags: array<string>, a: array<string>, aaaa: array<string>, caa: array<string>, cname: array<string>, dmarc: array<string>, mx: array<string>, ns: array<string>, spf: array<string>, srv: array<string>, txt: array<string>, soa: array<{name: string, ns: string, mailbox: string, serial: integer, refresh: integer, retry: integer, expire: integer, min_ttl: integer}>, score: number}>, pagination: {limit: integer, has_next: boolean, has_prev: boolean, next_token: string, prev_token: string, total_count: integer, query: string}}
  400: Invalid query parameters → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  500: Internal server error → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

### GET /v3/search/advanced/urls
Summary: Full-text search URL results
Description: Performs linguistic full-text search across URL result fields with BM25 relevance scoring.  **Search Features:** - Full-text search with stemming and linguistic analysis - Typo tolerance with fuzzy matching - BM25 relevance scoring (results ordered by relevance) - Efficient cursor-based pagination (O(1) performance)  **Query Examples:** - `?q=wordpress admin` - Find URL results containing wordpress OR admin - `?q="exact phrase"` - Find exact phrase matches - `?q=jquery&fields=script_urls,raw` - ...
Parameters:
  query q: string [required] — Search query - Full-text search across fields
  query fields: string — Comma-separated fields to search (default: all fields) - Example: raw,detection,script_urls
  query limit: integer — Results per page (default: 20, max: 100)
  query search_after: string — Pagination token from previous response (base64-encoded string)
  query url_host: string — Filter by URL host - Example: example.com
  query url_port: string — Filter by URL port - Example: 443
  query url_domain: string — Filter by URL domain - Example: example.com
  query remote_host: string — Filter by remote host IP - Example: 192.168.1.1
  query remote_port: string — Filter by remote port - Example: 443
  query favicon_mmh3: string — Filter by favicon MMH3 hash - Example: -1234567890
  query script_urls: string — Filter by script URL - Example: jquery.min.js
  query min_date: string — Filter by minimum checked_at date - ISO 8601 format: 2024-01-01T00:00:00Z
Responses:
  200: Search results with pagination metadata → {data: array<{url: string, url_host: string, url_port: string, url_domain: string, url_scheme: string, url_path: string, url_query: string, checked_at: ### GET /v3/search/hosts/{host}/summary
Summary: Get summary of port statuses by host
Parameters:
  path host: string [required] — Host
Responses:
  200: Summary of Port Statuses → {data: {ports: array<string>, services: array<string>, software: array<string>, certs: array<string>, keys: array<string>, details: array<{port: string, service: string, software: string, last_seen: string}>}, links: {}}
  400: Invalid Host → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  404: No Results Found → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

## Streams (3 endpoints)

### GET /v3/stream/scans
Summary: Stream live scan results (all jobs)
Description: Opens a WebSocket for live scan results across all jobs you can access.  **Connect** — `wss://api.efflux.io/v3/stream/scans`  **Authentication** — `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: efflux.v1, <token>`  **Command-line example:** `websocat -t -k 'wss://api.efflux.io/v3/stream/scans' -H="Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: efflux.v1, ${TOKEN}"`  **After connect** — `connected` message, then live envelopes (`scan`, `dns`, `url`, `check`). Live-only; use `GET /scans/{job_id}/results` for history.  **Raw payloads** — scan...
Parameters:
  query tenant_view: boolean — Tenant-wide scope (requires tenant privileges)
  query user_id: string — Filter to a tenant member's jobs
  query raw_limit: integer — Max bytes per raw string in scan/url results; omit raw when unset or 0; capped at server max
  header Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: string [required] — efflux.v1, <token>
  header Origin: string — Browser origin; not required for non-browser clients
Responses:
  101: Switching Protocols — first server message → {job_id: string, protocol: string, scope: string, type: string}
  401: Missing or invalid subprotocol/token → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  403: Insufficient permissions or origin not allowed → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

### GET /v3/stream/scans/{job_id}
Summary: Stream live scan results (single job)
Description: Opens a WebSocket for live scan results for one job. Same auth and keepalive as `/stream/scans`.  **Connect** — `wss://api.efflux.io/v3/stream/scans/{job_id}`  **Authentication** — `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: efflux.v1, <token>`  **Command-line example:** `websocat -t -k 'wss://api.efflux.io/v3/stream/scans/{job_id}' -H="Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: efflux.v1, ${TOKEN}"`  **Tenant scope** — callers with `scans:read_tenant` (or `tenant_admin`) automatically stream any job in their tenant. Optional `tenan...
Parameters:
  path job_id: string [required] — Job ID
  query tenant_view: boolean — Optional; by-id streams auto-elevate when caller has tenant read privileges
  query raw_limit: integer — Max bytes per raw string in scan/url results; omit raw when unset or 0; capped at server max
  header Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: string [required] — efflux.v1, <token>
  header Origin: string — Browser origin; not required for non-browser clients
Responses:
  101: Switching Protocols → {job_id: string, protocol: string, scope: string, type: string}
  200: Example live event envelope → {data: object, job_id: string, pipeline: string, region: string, tenant_id: string, type: string, user_id: string}
  401: Missing or invalid subprotocol/token → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  403: Insufficient permissions or origin not allowed → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  404: Job not found or not accessible → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}

### GET /v3/stream/status
Summary: Stream live resource status changes
Description: Opens a WebSocket for live status changes (jobs, captures, tasks, surveys, permutations).  **Connect** — `wss://api.efflux.io/v3/stream/status`  **Authentication** — `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: efflux.v1, <token>`  **Command-line example:** `websocat -t -k 'wss://api.efflux.io/v3/stream/status' -H="Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: efflux.v1, ${TOKEN}"`  **Filter by type** — optional `type=job,capture` (aliases: `scan` → `job`). Omit to receive all types.
Parameters:
  query type: string — Comma-separated resource types: job, capture, task, survey, permutation
  query tenant_view: boolean — Tenant-wide scope (requires tenant privileges)
  query user_id: string — Filter to a tenant member's status events
  header Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: string [required] — efflux.v1, <token>
  header Origin: string — Browser origin; not required for non-browser clients
Responses:
  101: Switching Protocols → {job_id: string, protocol: string, scope: string, type: string}
  200: Example status change → {id: string, status: string, tenant_id: string, time: string, type: string, user_id: string}
  401: Missing or invalid subprotocol/token → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
  403: Insufficient permissions or origin not allowed → {type: string, title: string, status: integer, detail: string, instance: string, errors: array<{field: string, message: string}>, rate_limit: {limit: integer, remaining: integer, reset_at: string, retry_after_seconds: integer}}
